38:["$","p",null,{"children":["Second, an efficiency one: all the on-site interaction terms are diagonal in the occupation basis and mutually commute, so they can share a single group and be synthesized together. The same holds for the bonds that touch no common site. On a chain, every second bond is such a set: bonds 0 and 2 act on disjoint pairs of modes, so their hopping terms commute and can share a group, and bond 1 forms a group of its own. If you group the even and the odd bonds, you therefore need three groups, where one group per bond needs four. 6b:["$","p",null,{"children":"The circuit carries no truncation, so the circuit itself does not change when the cutoff does: only the transpilation is repeated.
